# Master Blog Internal Linking Blueprint (251 Articles)
**Client:** Oraanj Interior Design & Build — London  
**Architecture Purpose:** Strategic Internal Linking Architecture for all 251 blog posts in `post-sitemap.xml`.  
**Implementation Standard:** Each published blog post contains **14 targeted internal links** (within the 10–15 link benchmark):
1. **6 Peer Blog Links** (Horizontal topical cluster authority across sibling articles)
2. **Primary Service Link** (High commercial intent core pillar service)
3. **Secondary Specialist Link** (Supporting specialist technical capability)
4. **Supporting Turnkey / CAD / Render Link** (Full-lifecycle service link)
5. **Primary London Location Page** (High-authority local borough/district SEO)
6. **Secondary London Location Page** (Adjacent London district authority)
7. **Primary Portfolio Case Study** (Niche-aligned proof of craftsmanship & conversion)
8. **Secondary Portfolio Case Study** (Complementary luxury project case study)
9. **Conversion Consultation Hub** (Call to action / free bespoke design consultation)

---

## Strategic Distribution & Anti-Clustering Architecture
To ensure seamless user experience and maximum algorithmic benefit without triggering Google link spam / clustering filters:
- **Spacing:** Minimum 100–180 words between successive internal links.
- **Section Allocation:**
  - *Introduction / Hook (1–2 links):* Primary Service or High-Level Peer Blog.
  - *Core Conceptual Body (4–6 links):* 3–4 Peer Blogs + Specialist Technical Service + Primary Location.
  - *Practical Execution / Specification (3–4 links):* Supporting Turnkey/CAD Service + Secondary Location + Primary Portfolio Case Study.
  - *Conclusion & Next Steps (2–3 links):* 1 Peer Blog + Secondary Portfolio Case Study + Consultation Hub CTA.

---
